8. TRANSFORMING OTT DIGITAL PLATFORM BUSINESS USING BLOCKCHAIN TECHNOLOGY

Blockchain technology provides advantages to OTT digital platform business in many ways. Some of the key features of blockchain for OTT digital platform business are listed below:

8.1. Providing Tokens for Audio and Video

With the adoption of blockchain, people can buy and sell assets using cryptocurrencies and so is copyright content too.

8.2. Provides Scope for New Artists and New Entrants

Blockchain creates new artists and fans in OTT platforms. It establishes direct communication between customers and artists.

8.3. Targeted Advertisements and Marketing

Blockchain helps in marketing by studying the current market situation and provide advertisements to the targeted customers easily. It provides transparency in marketing and advertising.

8.4. Royalty Payments and Smart Contracts

With the adoption of blockchain, royalty payments for artists will be transparent and hassle-free. Fig. (4) shows the royalty payment through blockchain.

8.5. The Decentralized Model of Content Delivery

Blockchain eliminates the centralized mode of authority for transactions, making them easier for peers to verify transactions directly.

8.6. Reduce Production Costs

Blockchain helps in making visual effects and rendering effects to movies at low cost and hence reducing the total production cost.

8.7. Micropayments

Blockchain promotes the use of digital currencies. By using the cryptocurrency wallet, the users can track their monthly expenses. Royalty payments can be directly paid to the copyright owners.

Fig. (4))
Royalty payment through Blockchain.

8.8. Licensing and Monetization

Blockchain can provide a license to small and medium-range content creators who are unable to afford heavy amounts charged from the big production houses. It permits only authorized users to track their royalty payments.

8.9. Royalty Tracking and Payments

Blockchain ensures that all the participants get their royalty payments for the revenue generated with it. Blockchain provides digital watermarking against theft and unauthorized usage of the content. Blockchain provides effective transactions by decentralized and distributed networks and also enables peer network participants to verify their transactions.

8.10. Blockchain-Powered Cloud Space

Blockchain can be used as a decentralized system that provides space for decentralized apps. This space can be explored and used by the users for peer-peer cloud storage and data delivery network. Video advertisements are the special interests of blockchain applications. Leveraging blockchain will provide significant benefits to the advertising industry by identifying potential customers.

9. BLOCKCHAIN TECHNOLOGY - DISRUPTIONS AS A GAME- HANGER IN OTT PLATFORM BUSINESS AND REAL-TIME APPLICATIONS IN DIGITAL ADVERTISING

Blockchain has changed the phase of the music and entertainment industry. Recent reports state that Blockchain in the telecom and media market is expected to grow from USD 46.6 million in 2018 to USD 993.8 million by 2023 as per the Compound Annual Growth Rate (CAGR) of 84.4% [26]. Different versions of blockchain projects are in progress for video streaming. Some of the disruptions of blockchain technology in OTT include:

9.1. Opportunities for new channel dispatches

New channels for live occasions, sports, dreams, sci-fi, and news are probably going to be made. Blockchain will empower players to manufacture a stage that will step by step become an aggregator for content creators and the decentralized stage will encourage the situating of new shows. This will remove the need for a streaming device.

9.2. Advertising and free substance

Any plan of action in the M&E business is vulnerable to interruption by the free substance. Video ventures that are Blockchain-empowered are probably going to be exchanged through cryptographic forms of money that can be utilized by advertisers for channelizing advancements. Blockchain will guarantee that crypto-currencies are credited legitimately to the content creators without any intermediaries.

10. Real-Time applications of Blockchain Technology in Digital Advertising and OTT Platform Business

There is a lot of focus on blockchain applications in digital advertising for OTT platforms. Premion, a leading OTT advertising platform, and Comcast Multichannel Video Programming Distributor are examples of OTT advertising platforms. For transactions, Premion has partnered with MadHive that is built on blockchain technology. Premion, MadHive, and AdLedger as a blockchain consortium have started Campaign Reconciliation that is based on a proof-of-concept (POC).

10.1. Visual Content and Media

Ethereum based, FilmChain gathers, designates, and breaks down incomes in film, TV, and other digital media. FilmChain encourages transparency in the distribution process without third-party intervention. It also explores ways for global funding and promotes reaching global audiences. StreambedMedia is a Toronto-based company that is making content provenance mechanisms allowing creators to track content on social networking sites like YouTube, Instagram, etc. This platform allows for sharing information with other content creators and links the users to the creators for payments.

10.2. Block Graph TM

BlockGraphTM formerly known as FreeWheel deployed blockchain with premium content across digital and linear TV. BlockGraphTM will empower advertisers, developers, and administrators to plan, target, and report on promotion purchases across computerized, communicate, and streaming music utilizing blockchain technology. Using BlockGraphTM, advertisers can secretly coordinate their data set with developers, administrators, and others in the business to target or analyze buyers on any gadget without giving up sensitive client data [27].

10.3. Music

Backed by Sony and BMG, Vezt is a company that holds the copyrights of music. Fans can share the royalties of the songs. Vezt attempts to go over the music business by giving specialists, lyricists, and creators with sponsoring sourced really from fans. Royalties are encoded and are followed on-chain. Fans are offered to purchase music of their choice. Another player, Verifi handles the rights of the music creators. Verifi provides synchronize ownership across music companies. Audius, supported by Lightspeed Ventures, General Catalyst, and Kleiner Perkins, means to be the blockchain-based adversary to Soundcloud. Audius offers free encouragement for its artists, presents 90% of salaries back to the producer, and ensures extended discoverability and fan responsibility for top-tier authorities [28].

10.4. Engaging Audiences

After collaborating with Ripple's XSpring, Scooter Braun, and Warner Music Group, Audigent has gained popularity in fundraising for advertisers in entertainment, sports, and lifestyle space. YellowHeart is the first ticketing platform built on a blockchain. It allows event organizers to book a concert venue and set up rules for ticket management.

10.5. Current Scenario and Beyond

To transform the digital advertising supply chain, individual consumers also should be involved. Identity management is the core element of blockchain technology; therefore, consumers will play an important role in adopting the use of digital wallets. Many OTT platforms started direct-to-digital releases during the current pandemic situation in 2020; OTT platforms have started investing in upcoming films. Many digital premiers during COVID-19 were streamed by Amazon Prime Video. In June 2020, Disney+Hotstar announced digital releases of seven films on its platform worth Rs.100 crore. OTT platforms make money by focusing on direct-to-digital releases. New subscribers are the potential for the OTT platforms. To capture the Indian market, many OTT platforms have started investing huge amounts of money for providing digital content. Amazon Prime Video, Disney+Hotstar, and Netflix are some of the OTT players that have been aggressive in spending for content in India. Netflix has invested Rs.3,000 crore for original content during December 2019. Amazon Prime has doubled its investment as its viewership has grown six-fold in two years. Disney+Hotstar has more investment from its parent company Star India. The second big investment for Disney+Hotstar of Rs. 1,113 crore happened in April 2020. Star India and Star US property had invested Rs.1,066crore in the OTT platform for the 13th edition of the Indian Premier League (IPL) in March 2020 [29]. For example, myNK is a flagship product of MinersINC which is India’s first blockchain-based entertainment ecosystem that connects content creators with consumers directly over a secured network [30].

11. MACHINE LEARNING (ML) ADOPTION IN BLOCKCHAIN (BC) – RESEARCH ISSUES AND CHALLENGES

There are many research challenges and issues in ML and BC that are yet to be addressed. The open issues and challenges of ML adoption in BC are shown in Fig. (5).

11.1. Suitability

In a distributed environment, for untrusted sources, blockchain is a viable solution. If performance is a major concern, then, a simple database is a better option. Therefore, the usability and architecture of blockchain must be clearly understood for any application before deployment.

11.2. Infrastructure

For blockchain-based applications, the hardware and network infrastructure designed exclusively try to improve the performance. However, research is going on for major usage in major companies for the adoption of blockchain for their applications.

11.3. Privacy

Privacy is the key issue in blockchain as the data stored in the blockchain is available to all the nodes in the network. Private Blockchains provide controlled access and encryption for securing the data. However, the ML model's adoption of these limited data imposed barriers for predictions and analytics.

Fig. (5))
ML adoption in BC – Research Challenges and Open issues.

11.4. Memory

The size of the data and the new blocks are constantly added to the blockchain network. Consequently, the performance of the blockchain is also affected by an increase in chain size which creates memory constraints. Blockchain is immutable, and storage of data is a major concern in most of the implementations.

11.5. Implementation

For larger applications, implementing blockchain requires high bandwidth of internet connection for a large number of transactions. Therefore, adding of blocks and transactions needs to be controlled.

11.6. Security

In a decentralized network, blockchains are prone to security issues and hence, the consensus protocol may be compromised (in public blockchains). In private blockchains, each node is identified, and an appropriate consensus protocol is placed.

11.7. Quantum Resilience

The hashing calculations utilized by blockchains could before long be broken with quantum PCs. Fortunately, quantum computing offers chances to support the exhibition and security of blockchains. Quantum cryptography can strengthen the security of the blockchain network as quantum correspondence is verified inalienably (clients can't copy another client). It can scramble whole P2P correspondences and supplant traditional advanced marks in the blockchain network. Research is going on to design blockchain with quantum computing [31]. Similarly, blockchain tries to solve security issues in smart cities [32, 33].

CONCLUSION

This paper discussed OTT platforms and their business in real-time. Over-the-top (OTT) has changed how individuals sit in front of the TV, and in some regions, it may overtake broadcast, cable, and satellite. With more audiences getting attracted to use OTT platforms, the OTT providers need to rely on the security of the users. Blockchain technology provides a suitable solution for providing security and preventing piracy. This chapter explores the benefits and advantages of using blockchain technology for OTT platforms. Royalty payments are paid securely using smart contracts without any third-party intervention. Distributed ledger technology provides security and privacy of the transactions in Ethereum. Blockchain technology protects the copyrights of the owners against any third-party attacks. This chapter is intended for the audiences who are interested to know about recent developments in OTT and blockchain technology. Blockchain technology will revolutionalize the future of the digital platform market and will expand its usability across many sectors in the coming years.

Blockchain-based Cultivating Ideas for Growth: A New Agronomics Perspective



Vishakha1, Nikhil Sharma2, *, Bharat Bhushan2, Ila Kaushik3
1 HMR Institute of Technology & Management, Delhi, India
2 School of Engineering and Technology, Sharda University, Greater Noida, India
3 Krishna Institute of Engineering & Technology, Ghaziabad, U.P., India

Abstract

The development of nourishment and food is an essential requirement of nations for their affluence and welfare. Agriculture is the science and specialty of developing plants and livestock. In sedentary human civilization, agriculture plays a key role. The agribusiness needs to do a ton of work to keep up and fabricate the purchaser’s trust with regards to the food quality check. A blockchain-based agricultural arrangement holds a guarantee for the agro-business with its capacity to acquire clarity and transparency in the framework. Minimal effort agrarian protection plans are progressively seen as instruments for giving social assurance to the individuals influenced by floods or dry seasons. In assisting with the diminishing effects, they endure because of such occasions. Therefore, this chapter introduces the idea of blockchain technology, extensive literature survey, including a review of blockchain development in the nourishment sector followed by the cases in the food supply chain. This chapter presented various opportunities and an ideal architecture of how blockchain will function in the farming sector. Finally, the chapter concludes by featuring open research difficulties in this field.

Keywords: Agribusiness, Agriculture, Blockchain, Farming Insurances, Food Safety, Internet business of Agri Products, IoT, Nourishment Supply network, Smart Agriculture, Smart Contracts, Supply Chain, Traceability, Transaction Costs, Underwater fishing industry.

1. INTRODUCTION

Apart from cryptocurrencies, blockchain exhibit a wide variety of applications. The innovations in the field of blockchain are surely transforming numerous industries from banking to the healthcare sector and also the real estate business. Now research works are going on to implement the same in the agricultural sector. With the advancement in IoT applications, the technological domain, as well as financial sectors have acquired substantial growth in ecological observing applications to assess the quality of water, soil, and air. The world population is calculated to be 7.8 billion as of April 2020. With the continuous increase in population, there is a drastic increase in the demand for per-capita food.

Studies in 2014-15 showed that an adult has a 6.1 percent more calorie urge as compared to that of an adult in 1975-76. Increasing demand for food has remarkably affected the environment, which as a result has endangered many species. The destruction caused to the environment due to high food demand is in the form of deforestation, increased levels of carbon emission, etc. If the food supply chain is efficiently organized then there is a potential of reducing carbon footprint and environmental hazards which in turn will guarantee food safety [1]. In the current food supply chain, there are several stakeholders involved from production to consumption, making the situation difficult to retrieve actual step-by-step data for analysis. It is presented in the reports that in agricultural farming activities, a significant amount of carbon is produced in various processes. In the United States, it is estimated that among all the factors responsible for the generation of greenhouse gases, only transportation of food contributes 11% to the emission. It is observed that food production must be doubled by 2050 to meet the nutrients requirement of every individual [2].

Due to the increase in the cases of food consumption diseases, now the consumers want to know detailed information about their eatables and others. People show very little reliance on the existing food system. Blockchain has provided exciting solutions for these problems. It provides eternal evidence for each proceeding part which is then arranged into various blocks and no changes can be reflected there. It also helps in replacing those physical trackers and monitoring entities to prevent the imprecise impact of the traditional supply chain [3]. Tracking of the supply chain is the most important measure to protect the safety of eatables, securing them from malign vulnerabilities and encouraging food certification. LetsFarm is a start-up utilizing computerized reasoning with AI to enable ranchers over the globe. It utilizes transparent instruments and advancements to associate farmers so they can get the data they need from any place. Appropriate data can assist farmers with increasing their yield and to have a better salary from similar harvests that they are developing. LetsFarm likewise engages farmers with auxiliary salary by giving crucial data and expanding the pay of farmer’s family units. After all, the overall growth of ranchers is the progress of the country all in all!. The Dutch Ministry of Agriculture, Nature, and Food Quality financed the main research venture, “Blockchain for Agri-food” which has been proposed to investigate blockchain suggestions for Agri-food. Pilot research shows that blockchain innovation empowered nourishment to be followed from farm to market in only a couple of seconds [4]. Blockchain additionally assists with watching bottomless items and diminish instances of illicit gathering and delivery cheats. The United Nations uncovers that nourishment fakes cost the worldwide economy around $40 billion every year because of unlawful exchanges. A ton of advancements originated from new businesses and the rural division isn't a special case. Ripe.io and Pavo Coin are two ongoing business models that use blockchain in the supply chain [5].

Ripe.io is a new business situated in SanFrancisco. Ripo.io centers around farming and offers clients an elevated level of straightforwardness. The framework created by Ripo.io can distinguish mugginess, readiness, and the temperature of the item. In this way, the rancher gets the data about the states of things. Blockchain achieving decentralization is not entirely technical in nature rather it’s a combination of technical and smart engineering incentives. It began in 2008 with Satoshi Nakamoto, whose real identity is still not known to anyone, released a white paper that introduced a purely peer-to-peer version of crypto cash called bitcoin. There are four types of blockchain named as a private blockchain, public blockchain, federated blockchain, and the last one being hybrid. In private blockchain access is controlled, only a few users have the permission to view and verify the transaction. It is used in services where data security is a prime concern [6]. A public blockchain is one that has open access to all though transactions are anonymous. Transactions are transparent to those who have view access in the ledger. Federated blockchain is operated by a group or federation and only the members of the group have access. Hybrid refers to the system which uses public blockchain but also hosts private blockchain. Though data is stored on a public blockchain yet data privacy is not intruded. There is an incentive that is provided to the nodes for behaving honestly. There are two incentive mechanisms in the bitcoin named block reward and the other is transaction payment. In the service of creating a block in the blockchain, initially, the creator of the block includes the generation of special coin transactions in the block and then chooses the receiver address of this agreement. Block maker gets to the opportunity to gather the reward only if the block terminates up on long-term consensus branch. In the blockchain, the worth of block reward gets halved after every four years. Block reward is how new bitcoins are made and it is assessed that it will run out in 2140. There will be no new bitcoins if this standard remains unchanged. In transaction fees, the procedure creator of the transaction can decide to make the yield estimation of coin lesser than input esteem, and the rest of the amount will be the transaction expense to the creator of the block which is placed on the consensus branch [7]. There are many problems associated with these methods such as how to pick a random junction for each transaction or how to keep away every node from running the bitcoin node for collecting the reward. To improve upon this now bitcoins are generated by the process of consensus mechanism which includes various processes such as PoW, PoS, DPoS, and PBFT. In the Proof of Work (PoW) process, a bitcoin miner who finds the hash value first will be permitted to include another new block of exchange to the blockchain. In the Proof of Stake (PoS) mechanism, the more coins the stakeholder has, it is more likely that they will include a new block of exchange to the blockchain. In Delegated Proof of Stake (DPOS), coin holders can utilize their balance to choose a list of modes to be possibly permitted to add new transaction blocks to the blockchain. Practical Byzantine Fault Tolerance (PBFT) relies on no. of nodes to affirm trust. When enough feedbacks are obtained which in general is 51%, the transaction is declared as a valid transaction. There are mainly two important terminologies in the blockchain named as a consensus mechanism and smart contracts which need to be understood before studying the utility of it in the agricultural domain. Consensus Mechanism is a way to guarantee a mutual agreement on a data point. Each vertex in the system grid has a duplicate of a similar record(ledger). It is secured in nature and an economic framework of the cryptographic protocol. Objectives of a consensus mechanism include agreement seeking, collaboration, cooperation, inclusive property, and participation. There are three basic steps of consensus, the first one being endorsement which determines whether to accept a transaction or not. Then comes ordering, which sorts all the transactions into a series in a specified period. The last one is the validation which verifies the endorsement policy. Smart contracts, also known as a self-executing contract, is a computer convention that digitally facilitates, checks, or upholds the presentation of an agreement by avoiding middlemen interaction and validates it over a decentralized platform by all peers who stores the blockchain. It is used for the exchange of money, shares, etc. It is self-verifying, self-executing in nature, and has resistance to any change. It turns legal obligations into automated processes. It is cheaper as there are no third-party fees involved. It guarantees a greater degree of security.

The other sections of the chapter are standardized as follows, Section 2 presents a literature review, Section 3 describes different opportunities of blockchain technology in the agriculture field, Section 4 discusses the application of blockchain in agriculture, Section 5 illustrates use cases, Section 6 explains Blockchain-Based Architecture in Agriculture and challenges followed by the conclusion in Section 7.

2. LITERATURE SURVEY

In a traditional system, the seed company produces the seed which is identified according to global standards and updates the local record. The farmer buys the seed, harvests the crops, and sends it to the grain elevator which stores the produced crop in various warehouses. Various grain processors buy the grains from warehouses and refine them to make the end product. Then distributors take those end products and sell them to retailers who further sell the product in small identifiable quantities to the customer [8]. Agriculture in India can be categorized into various phases such as production, supply chain, processing, and marketing. Fig. (1) represents the various phases in the chain that lead to credit risk and limit the flow of credit due to poor supply of inputs and outdated farming policies in the production phase, high wastage, and poor transportation facility along with many intermediaries in supplying phase.

Fig. (1))
Phases of supply chain.

Buyers need to know the starting point of nourishment and they even can pay moreover to discover all essential data about the eatables. They can see food accreditation but regular confirmation can be misrepresented as seen in the present system. However, blockchain being immutable in nature will make it difficult to change any record subtly like date, and substance which will stay unaltered. A single scanning of a QR code will give a shopper all data about nourishment –i.e. which type of seeds are used for the production purpose, what pesticides are used in the process, how much time it is put in the shop etc. Similarly, it can be used for poultry purposes to know the exact time, what conditions it lived in, what infections it had, etc. Just with a single click, all the necessary information will reach the consumer [9].

The worldwide workforce is estimated to be 46%, out of which only the farming sector presents 8.4% of the whole world's monetary, and its overall creation is 9,084,800 million. Blockchain gives secure information trade and a carefully designed vault for these reports and delivery events which will save billions of dollars wasted due to the misinterpretation of data. Maersk and IBM are utilizing blockchain technology to make a worldwide carefully designed framework for digitizing the exchange work process and following shipments end-to-end, eliminating grindings including expensive point-to-point correspondences [10]. In the case of agriculture, India is one of the largest nations which constitute majorly for production purposes. Among all sectors, the primary sector of India contributes significantly to the economy though it is decreased down to 15% due to the continuous evolvement of industries and other commercial sectors. Fig. (2) shows the division of available land in India. India is the second biggest natural product maker on the planet, the assessed measure of cultivation creation is 314.7 million tonnes. Total horticulture sends out from India is evaluated to be US $38.54 billion [11].

A combination of machine learning concepts with blockchain innovation will boost up the economy in approaching years [12].

A remarkable part of the use cases examines the theoretical utilization of blockchain in horticultural supply chains missing the mark regarding specific usage structure or approach.

3. OPPORTUNITIES

There are numerous opportunities for blockchain technology in the nourishment sector [31, 32] such as procurement tracking, crop, and food production management, management of agricultural finances and insurances, increasing transparency, economic observation, and covering green bonds, etc.

3.1. Procurement Tracking

The major obstacle in the farming sector is to track the delivery and payout for the conveyance of food. These days, the procedure relies upon an outsider for planning the merchandise conveyance. The vendors as in the traditional supply system have an operator who guarantees that the products are conveyed securely and purchasers have a specialist to suggest settlement and review the conveyance. The participation of various operators adds significant expenses to the framework and makes the whole procedure tedious. With the help of blockchain technology, the entire procedure can be improved to a solitary distributed record.

3.2. Land Registry

In India, the United Nations Development Program is working with many associates and companions to make land vault increasingly solid there. At an elevated level, this venture will catch and invariably record every exchange all through the offer of a property. The Swedish government's territory proprietorship authority known as Lantmäteriet has directed land roster and property exchange on the blockchain. They believed that this project gives a secure approach to have digitalized authenticity and that it could diminish a huge number of dollars of costs for the administration. The Republic of Georgia is probing the utilization of the bitcoin system to approve property-related government exchanges.

3.3. Underwater Fishing Industry

The other use case is present in the underwater Fishing or Angling Industry where blockchain technology can be utilized to follow and stop illicit, uncontrolled, and unregistered fishing which represents the prominent danger to marine environments. In New Zealand, the World Wildlife Fund is dealing with a pilot venture to get rid of illicit angling and the maltreatment of human rights in the fish industry of the Pacific Islands.

3.4. Crop and Food Production Management

With the assistance of smart cultivation, IoT sensors could bring significant data like the water level, soil temperature, compost, and fertilizer particulars, etc., and direct it to the blockchain. It will support upgrading the nature of the cultivation procedure just as the quality of produced crops.

3.5. Climate Emergency Control

Farmers need to confront the issues concerning climate emergency control such as eccentric climatic conditions consistently. Because of excessive rainfalls, it gets hard for developed harvests to endure flooding. Purchasers are never informed of when did the harvests endure awful climatic conditions and for what reasons the market comes to terms with the high up surging prices. As it is feasible for the included individuals in the blockchain arrangement to detect the climate conditions, farmers can rapidly ask for and get protection guarantees with the help of smart agreements.

3.6. Management of Agricultural Finances and Insurances

Another use case lies with the management of agricultural finances and insurances. Lack of clarity in records and credit history poses significant among smallholders and financial institutions. Today, budgetary administrations don't just permit the small farmers to put resources into cultivation yet also help them in settling liquidity limitations. By using blockchain, the farming fund process turns out to be progressively transparent and reasonable and empowers shared control convenience.

3.7. Increasing the Transparency

The opportunity is consistently expanding. Transparency in farming is also considered as one of the important use cases. Barilla, Italian pasta, and pesto sauce maker have collaborated with IBM to handle detectability and transparency in its pesto creation cycle. Starting from the development stage, going through treatment and collecting in the field to transportation, stockpiling, quality control to the creation and afterward to the consumers, all particulars and features are followed and made accessible on a blockchain framework so that the client can check by scanning QR code of the pesto.

3.8. Economic Observation and Covering Green Bonds

This consists of economic observing, checking, and covering green bonds, which were made to finance the schemes and projects that will benefit the ecosystem. It is important to have credible trailing, recognizability, and confirmation components to help in improving the trust of financial investors in atmosphere smart activities. Carbon credits and exchanging them could make a profit by the belief that blockchain presents. There are numerous opportunities for blockchain technology in the nourishment sector such as procurement tracking, crop and food production management, management of agricultural finances and insurances, increasing transparency, economic observation and covering green bonds, etc.

4. APPLICATIONS

This segment consists of four classes of utilizations in agrarian and nourishment areas, namely smart cultivation, agricultural insurance, exchanges of agricultural items, and food supply network.

4.1. Smart Agriculture

Basics of the Agri-nourishment frameworks are the fundamental information and data on the natural assets that help all types of cultivation. Various stakeholders and actors produce and oversee information and data according to their requirements and limits. Smart agribusiness is highlighted by the use of IoT, ICT, and different present-day information assortment and examination techniques including sensors, unmanned flying vehicles, and AI. A key issue of setting up smart cultivation is building up a far-reaching security framework that encourages the utilization and the executives of information [33]. The blockchain innovation serves to store information and data that different stakeholders and farmers create all through the whole worth-added procedure, from seed sowing to trade, of delivering a farming item. It guarantees that the information and data are clear and unambiguous to the included stakeholders and farmers and every single registered information is changeless. Conventional ways control information in a centralized manner and are inclined to improper information, information mutilation, and abuse the same as a digital rack. For instance, ecological observing information is largely controlled by centralized administration substances that have their advantage [34]. They can control the conclusion related to information. Many smart cultivation models are presented and executed dependent on the joint use of blockchain and IoT innovations. Patil et al. suggest “a blockchain-dependent prototype which is lightweight for brilliant conservatory greenhouse farms”, where IoT sensors go about as a private blockchain that is controlled centrally by the proprietor.

4.2. Nourishment Supply Network

With expanded development and serious rivalry in the market, nourishment supply networks have become longer and more composite than any time in recent times. There are some regular issues in nourishment supply networks, for example, food quality and security, food tracing, nourishment trust, and inefficiency of the store network, which includes extra danger to the whole economy, society, and human health. From the makers' point of view, the utilization of blockchain innovation sets up a trusted association with customers and develop the notoriety of their items, by clearly giving data of individual item in the blockchain [35]. This would make it hard for providers of misrepresentation and low-quality items to remain in business sectors and enforces all providers to improve the nature of their products in the entire farming and nourishment parts. Undertakings can accomplish the estimation of their items and in this manner increment their competition in the market. It helps to address the purchasers' anxiety about the quality, security, and ecological accessibility of nourishment. The utilization of blockchain makes it possible for the producers and purchasers to associate with each other since customers can comprehend the nourishment creation process easily and in a more characteristic way. The current blockchain innovation in the food store network is still in the beginning phase of improvement. All stakeholders, including makers, purchasers, and government administrative bodies, would thus be able to be profited [36]. For instance, the DNA of domesticated animals, pesticide build-ups of vegetables, or grain can be checked and confirmed by any stakeholder engagement with the supply network of the item. In the current time, the gathering of such information for all items can lead to higher costs, yet for the testing phase, it tends to be done on the sample test. Alibaba, JD.com, and Wal-Mart are effectively executing blockchain detectability ventures and utilizing it to follow the whole procedure of foodstuff creation, preparation, and deals.

4.3. Internet Business of Agri Products

The internet business and exchange of rural items face some vital issues to explain. On-spot cash on conveyance along with management administration are the most vital difficulties looked at by internet business organizations, particularly in developing nations like India. But this difficulty is not creating hindrances anymore due to the setups of e-commerce organizations like Grofers, big basket, etc. Moreover, internet business retailers likewise need to deal with small orders including various diversified items that require more time which causes high working expenses for web-based business organizations. A business venture of Hubei locality in India, Old Farmers' Shopping Mall which is a web-based business organization, can be followed back to the source and all the creation data is a questionable asset by customers. Before the products are placed in the stores, various detailed data such as seed sowing, irrigation information, preparation, and fertilizing are recorded. They additionally give fundamental information on makers, transportation coordinators, stockpiling days, and stock-keeping temperature [37]. Clients just need to filter the QR code on the products and all the data will be accessible to them. This strategy can successfully keep away from the phony of illicit traders, middlemen, and remake customer’s trust in farm items from online businesses and their providers. Blockchain innovation may give appropriate answers for some parts of these issues such as data security. It gives private key encryption which is an incredible asset that gives the confirmation prerequisites. It would thus be able to connect the information of all parts of sowing and collection of rural items securely and unchangeably. The blockchain furnishes a computerized payment arrangement with zero rates. Besides, the utilization of digital currency in the exchange of agricultural items will lessen exchange costs more significantly. It also reduces the expenses of cultivators. Numerous rural items are delivered by family units but due to the low exchange volume and little scope, customary internet businesses are not willing to offer assistance for them, accordingly barring these members from the market. Blockchain can significantly lessen exchange costs and consolidate them into the market once more.

4.4. Farming Insurances

Climate limits endanger agrarian creation, putting nourishment security in danger where both the harvest and animals’ creation are influenced, and environmental change is required to additionally worsen climatic conditions in the future. Agrarian protection plans are generally an all-around perceived approach to predict and perceive the climate-related dangers in advance. Here, ranchers pay protection fees before the crop season starts and get protection pay if they experience a misfortune on their farmland. In this way, the safety net provider bears all the safeguarded hazards and cultivators can deal with their budgetary presentations to climatic conditions. Besides, complete insurance inclusion with no deducible amounts is conceivable and installments can be made convenient and automated soon after an unfavorable climate occasion is estimated. Errors among pay-out and on-farm misfortune can happen which is indicated as premise chance. Three sources of premise hazard can happen named spatial premise hazard, temporal basis hazard, and plan premised hazard. The spatial premise hazard denotes any contrasts among estimated and the on-farm climate through spatial separation technique. Temporal or fleeting premise hazard defines that an uncertain time window is picked for list assurance as depicted that instead of evaluating developing season precipitation procedure calculates entire year precipitation. Plan premise hazard abridges every other remaining single residual source such as missing climatic factors or one-sided specialized applications [38].

Cultivation protections contrast with how misfortunes are evaluated and thusly how payments are activated. Protections that get reimbursed to the ranchers dependent on a destruction appraisal that was made by a specialist on the farmland are signified as repayment-based protections. Repayment-based protections can cover misfortunes, but in some cases, they are inclined to issues emerging from deviated data issues. Ranchers are needed to be better educated about both unfavorable choices and moralized hazard issues. A Swiss-based organization Etherisc gives decentralized harvest protection dependent on blockchain innovation that gives pay-outs dependent on climatic information in decentralized insurance protocol token mechanism as local cash. Another protection supplier is situated in New York City, World Cover, which gives index protection agreements to small cultivators in Ghana, recreated the utilization of an Ethereum blockchain-based keen agreement in which pay-outs would be made in the digitalized currency Ether. Another brilliant harvest protection supplier is Arbol where a rancher can suggest an agreement that incorporates the excellent installment, a pay-out, and a climate occasion that triggers the pay-out, and then a financial specialist, as counterparty can consent to that proposed agreement.

Installments can be made convenient and machine-dependent on climate information that triggers the pay-out as characterized in a smart agreement. Secondly, climate data and other information sources such as plant development data or information gathered by farm apparatus can be naturally incorporated using a brilliant oracle improving premise chance reduction which makes the recorded assurance and pay-out process progressively effective. The utilization of blockchain innovation in web-based business and exchange of agricultural items is still in its earliest stages and the present case isn't just great as how to guarantee the legitimacy of the transferring procedure of information into blockchain is as yet an issue and a question to the blockchain developers. Later on, a prospective alternative might be IoT [39]. Also, blockchain's qualities of conveyed, distributed, non-tamper-able, recognizable needs to be more generally and profoundly investigated to improve the profitability proficiency and productivity of farming creation and exchange.

5. USE CASES

With the emergence of the various prospectus in the field of blockchain, a variety of applications have been studied and implemented side by side [40]. Various use cases of agriculture include food safety, supply chain traceability, transaction cost, opening new markets, and logistics.

5.1. Food Safety

It is by all accounts the territory where the most work has just been done because there is a clear personal stake from both maker and shopper. IBM alongside organizations like Walmart has begun driving the charge in this limit. Carrying transparency or clarity to the food supply chain will permit us to distinguish and expel awful entertainers and poor procedures. This guarantees perfect conditions from farming land to market, and we can pinpoint the source rapidly in case of a food sanitation breakout. This could spare time, cash, and lives.

5.2. Traceability

The advantage of purchasing localized food is constantly depicted as “you most likely are aware of where your food originates from and who developed it and it's fresh”. Imagine a scenario where we could get this going at scale. This means, regardless of the place where you purchased your food, you knew where it originated from, however when it was reaped and prepared, and even who delivered it. Organizations like Ripe.io are attempting to take care of this very issue. This could likewise go far to forestall food extortion, erroneous marking, and repetitive agents.

5.3. Transaction Costs

It is both divided and reliant on specifically knowing a counter-party before you could confide in them to work together. Organizations like AgriDigital are making progress in making transparent and productive stock and supply chains using blockchain innovation. They are applying the innovation legitimately to the grain exchange and plan to venture into other horticultural wares, for example, cotton.

5.4. Opening New Markets

It is another use case in this field. In a future digital recording scene, Agri Ledgeris opening new markets to farmers in the ongoing developing world utilizing blockchain. The reason here is that on the off chance that we can make trust and responsibility among advertising players, there is a decreased need to assess every individual separately on their reliability and capacity to execute. This implies advertise players that couldn't set up a trust before in any way, shape, or form as they didn't reside near one another, they didn't have a convention for if things self-destructed, an opportunity to build up another relationship didn't legitimize the worth, and so on. They presently could work together without somebody expecting to handle trust (and take an edge) in the center. This additionally implies that impeded market members can have a kind of “seat at the table” through this innovation.

5.5. Logistics

Any individual who has worked in the horticultural supplying chain knows the difficulties faced due to lack of coordination. Managing items in large quantities that frequently have a short period of usability is questionable with a ton of dollars on the line. Additionally, the supply network can be unsettled. Organizations like UPS are joining the Blockchain in transport alliance to assume a primary job in the keen coordination system of things to come, based on blockchain innovation. As this is already emerging in various other industries, the cultivation industry is going to be the next in this chain. Due to extensive research, many applications have been implemented in this domain, and further, a lot of researches is being continuously made for improving the existing limitations. Many start-ups in this domain such as FARMS, Agri-Digital, Ag-Unity, TE-Foods, Hi Tractor, Agri-ledger, Shamba, etc. are doing well in this field. FARMS stands for Financial and Agricultural Risk Management for Smallholders. Along with increasing a farmer’s economical literacy, it also teaches the various ways of risk management.

The FARMS idea is empowered by a blockchain-based virtual cash stage incorporated with satellite information and versatile cash arrangements, which guarantees straightforward secure exchanges and “reserving” of assets, mechanized installments, and data dashboards. Farmers deal with the virtual money known as “dry spell coins” that are kept in their own COIN22 versatile wallet account. All the exchanges are now only a click away and can be checked at any random time through SMS. Agri-Digital is a blockchain for the food supply chain. For global-based industries, it provides cloud-based products and solutions for their management. It creates a link between grain producers, purchasers, financers through a solitary stage, permitting them to contract, convey, and make installments safely and progressively.

In Kenya, IBM in partnership with a Nigerian start-up Hello Tractor has started blockchain cultivating applications. It is a rural innovation organization that associates tractor proprietors to smallholder farmers who needs tractor benefits through an Internet of Things innovation. Their innovation makes a successful win circumstance for both the tractor proprietor and farmers as tractor owners can productively spread their tractor employing business and farmers can conveniently access tractor services for cultivation.

AgUnity has built up an infrastructure that is giving a pathway to monetary incorporation for the world's underprivileged farmers. It is straightforward portable assistance that enables little farmers to plan, exchange, and track regular exchanges. This is a path for ranchers to collaborate, earn, save money for the future, and effectively purchase items and services. The application guarantees that whenever small-scale farmers give their crops to other farmers or lend farming tools, everyone involved in the process gets fairly paid.

TE-FOOD is the world's biggest openly available, farm-to-table nourishment traceability arrangement. It serves 400,000 business exchanges and handles 6000+ business clients every day. It introduces nourishment history to the purchasers. The blockchain record contains the evidence of the data entered by the distinctive production network members and information can't be distorted by a nourishment organization. Shamba Records is a Kenya-based Agri-tech start-up. It has manufactured a blockchain-based stage that utilizes artificial intelligence and enormous information to gather farmers harvesting records, process installments, etc. It provides many features to the users such as information assortment and mapping, smart agreements, etc. making farms progressively proficient and gathering data that can permit them to get to money-related administrations.

6. BLOCKCHAIN-BASED ARCHITECTURE IN AGRICULTURE AND CHALLENGES

Smart contract plays an effective role in agricultural assurance, food traceability, and green bonds. The architecture of blockchain implemented agricultural activities can be studied in two phases. The first phase shows the collection and cleaning of data and storing it in the smart contracts as shown in Fig. (5). The various stages of agriculture include soil and crop monitoring, storage, and distribution system of eatables and marketing as shown in Fig. (5). A lot of data is required to be collected to maintain the quality of the supply chain and to establish the faith of customers in blockchain technology. In the soil monitoring system, data such as pH, temperature, and amount of other minerals of soil should be stored. The number of pesticides, fertilizers used and the water used for the irrigation purpose needs to be stored which is now possible using the sensors such as IoT devices. Various weather controls need to be noted down such as wind speed, moisture content, barometric pressure, precipitation, ultraviolet radiation, etc. In terms of storage and distribution of entities data such as quality control labels, transportation information using sensors, and data related to the security of food during the inspection of warehouses need to be entered [41]. In the marketing procedure, the bill of the data including the taxes according to the renowned norms should be there in the system. At this point, the way toward cleaning and advancement of the gathered information will happen to guarantee that it is organized and reasonable, before saving it on the blockchain. Data identified with timestamp, demography, and type will be added to the information for making it increasingly organized [42]. Meeting consistent guarantees that the recognizable data related to the information gathered from IoT gadgets is ensured and follows safety efforts. When the information is advanced, it is placed into the AI-prepared format. Data will be made increasingly quickly with AI calculations. After the data processing step, they are going to be processed using a smart contract and get stored there to avoid any turbulence and changes.

Fig. (5))
Phase 1 of blockchain-based architecture in agriculture.

The second phase of the architecture involves the hashing and utilization of various algorithms to further identify the utilization of blockchain in agriculture as shown in Fig. (6). After the storing of data in smart contracts, it will divide into blocks so that hashing of the data block takes place. Now the data is stored in various blocks with their respective hash functions. This data is now become immutable and secured, and it can be utilized to track down the supply chain, calculate acquired land for production purposes, reduce the amount of resource wastage in natural disasters such as floods and drought, etc. It will also help the financial system and customers to keep an audit on the recorded prices. The people who want to invest their money in agricultural land will be benefitted as they will know the quality of soil and other factors beforehand and can make decisions [43]. Customers who are buying food from the market can ensure the quality and nutrients well in advance and if there is a defect in eatables, a systematic investigation can be easily achieved in considerably less amount of time as data is present in the blockchain and it can’t be modified.

Fig. (6))
Phase 2 of blockchain-based architecture in agriculture.

Blockchain technology will secure the farmers from crop failure situations by predicting in advance the trends of weather and other climatic conditions. Linking of sensors with the distributed ledger will result in the continuous updating of the records in real-time. It will result in the increased efficiency and a fair payment system for the farmers. After the implementation of blockchain, the agriculture sector too will become transparent and can detect fraud to prevent the situation of black marketing. It will also attain the attraction of investors. It will also result in the exchange of stocks between the farmers and in the future can introduce cryptocurrency in the agricultural domain.

Regardless of a large number of potential favorable benefits, some key restrictions stay for applying blockchain innovation in farming and nourishment sectors [44].

  • First, a lot of further research is required to give authentic and exact data to the blockchain record. This may be particularly significant on account of smallholder cultivation. The data produced in the cultivation procedure is dispersed and claimed by individual farmers. Blockchain innovations' advantages for farmers are highly reliant on the size of the farming land.
  • Second, transferring information on the blockchain can be expensive, which will be an obstruction to the appropriation of blockchain innovation in the area. The introduction of blockchain technology should be step by step, starting from the resource planning corporate, storage house management, and manufacturing industries.
  • Third, blockchain doesn't incorporate existing inheritance frameworks directly. To be effectively actualized, the innovation should be connected to a current database and heritage frameworks. For example, industry asset planning and assembling execution frameworks.
  • Building an establishment to use blockchain development is routinely tedious.
  • The arrangement of administrative structure around blockchain is in its earliest stages and thus involves high danger. Various blockchain ventures, particularly those managing money or cross-fringe exchanges, requires KYC/AML consistency and it is critical to comprehend the national structure before digging into these activities. The regular changes in the cost of bitcoin and news regarding the hacking of digital currency have brought expanded regulations and guidelines by various nations involving the banning of cryptocurrency in some countries.
  • The other disadvantages include high energy requirement as the data can be of size 100-200 gigabytes which require a large amount of energy for processing. Less speed of transactions is also a major drawback.

CONCLUSION & FUTURE SCOPE

Food well-being is a significant duty of the legislature and administrative specialists. In this manner, the administration needs to make a cross-industry or interdepartmental expert framework to successfully control the nourishment production network. The food supply chain based on blockchain technology can be traced from the farms to the retailer’s shop or groceries within a second. Blockchain has immense potential to transform agriculture, which is a massive industry though it won’t be seen as glamorous as in the healthcare sector or finance system. In this work, we overviewed the different perspectives of blockchain technology, advancement in the farming sector with the utilization of blockchain, and introduced a perfect model of how blockchain will work in the cultivation division. Different recent research work in this area has been surveyed, which gives tremendous opportunities for blockchain innovation in agriculture. A remarkable part of the use cases examines the theoretical utilization of blockchain in horticultural supply chains missing the mark regarding specific usage structure or approach. Despite the advantages of blockchain technology, there are still some challenges in advancing the blockchain innovation for helping the administrations track, screen, and review the food chain and helping producers to record the exchanges authentically and transparently. Blockchain and man-made reasoning are set to assume endlessly noteworthy jobs in the food supply business, remembering for nourishment security and broadening, state industry, and innovation specialists. It is of extraordinary essentiality to improve the administrative guidelines of the nourishment production network, to improve administration level and proficiency, to forestall lease chasing, and to breed defilement. Implementation of blockchain in agriculture involves many benefits as it will ensure the improved quality of the supply chain. It increases traceability as the consumers know the exact location where their eatables are produced. PavoCoin is an IoT-based software platform that is built on the PavoCoin framework, based on Ethereum that helps ranchers in the procedure of harvest development. Besides, this arrangement can likewise gather information about social occasions, handling, and dissemination of the item. Because of the usage of blockchain innovation, the procedures can be effectively checked and the data is put away. Blockchain innovation carries plenty of creative answers for farming. Blockchain-based programming let farmers improve regular procedures and increase the income of the current business.
